    CHAIRMAN’S LETTER
    主席信札




     Li Xuechun 李學純
                 Chairman 主席




    Dear Shareholders,                                                       各位尊敬的股東：

    During the year, in light of the efforts made by all staff under the     這一年，在董事會和管理層的正確帶領和全
    leadership of the Board and management, we spared no effort in           體幹部員工的奮力拼搏下，我們全力克服了
    overcoming various impacts caused by the COVID pandemic and              疫情帶來的各種影響，成功應對了突如其來
    successfully dealt with the unexpected dual controls over energy         的 能 耗 雙 控，化 風 險 為 機 遇，把 困 難 變 成 了
    consumption and carbon emission, thereby turning risks into              墊 腳 石，不 僅 確 保 了 企 業 正 常 生 產 經 營，而
    opportunities, and challenges into stepping stones. We not only          且積極引領和主導了市場，企業發展雲帆高
    ensured our normal production and operation activities, but also         張，捷報頻傳，一片生機勃勃。
    actively led and dominated the market. As a result, our business
    development has been in remarkable progress with various favorable
    achievements, demonstrating a dynamic and vibrant development
    trend.

    For the year ended 31 December 2021, the audited turnover of the         本集團截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止經
    Group amounted to approximately RMB21.5 billion, representing            審 計 的 營 業 額 約 為 人 民 幣 215 億 元，較 二 零
    an increase of approximately 29% as compared to 2020. Profit             二零年上升約 29%。股東應佔溢利約為人民
    attributable to Shareholders amounted to approximately RMB1.3            幣 13 億元，按年上升約 103%。全年股息（包
    billion, representing a year-on-year increase of approximately 103%.     括中期股息及末期股息）21.5 港仙（2020 年全
    Dividends for 2021 (including interim and final dividends) amounted to   年股息 9.9 港仙），維持穩定的 35% 派息率。
    HK21.5 cents (dividends for 2020: HK9.9 cents) maintaining a stable
    dividend payout ratio of 35%.

a region with abundant corn resources, in recent years.

increasing prices of raw materials were usually passed on to the            格的上漲一般會經過一段時間傳導給下游客

products including MSG, animal nutrition, high-end amino acid, and          給 下 游 客 戶，主 要 產 品 味 精、動 物 營 養、高
xanthan gum rising across the board during the year (especially the         檔氨基酸、黃原膠等全面漲價（尤其是第四季
fourth quarter), we successfully passed on the rising cost pressure to      度），從而保持了盈利能力。
our downstream clients, thus maintaining our profitability.

In terms of our product portfolio, our key products can be classified       從我們的產品組合來看，我們的主要產品分
into two categories, namely, cash cow products and star products.           為 兩 大 類 別，即 金 牛 產 品 及 明 星 產 品。金 牛
Our cash cow products include MSG and xanthan gum products                  產品包括味精及黃原膠，我們佔據該兩項產
and hold leading positions in the global market, of which we pursue         品的全球市場領導地位，我們的策略是在現
the strategy to maximise our revenue with our existing market share.        有市場份額下，實現收入的最大化。也就是說，
In other words, we will not wage price wars to increase our market          我們不會用價格戰來提升市場份額。我們的
share. Our star products, including animal nutrition product (such as       明星產品包括動物營養產品（蘇氨酸及賴氨酸

to its high growth potential, our high-end amino acid products enjoy        的高檔氨基酸產品不但具備高速增長潛力，

and animal nutrition products at the product application level so           面與我們的食品添加劑及動物營養產品產生
as to optimise our collection of food additives and animal nutrition        協同效應，使我們的食品添加劑及動物營養
products.                                                                   產品系列更為完善。

    competitive market. Looking back, we believe the right decision           們當年的決定是正確的，動物營養分部已經
    was made, as the animal nutrition segment has become one of our           成為我們最主要的業務之一。從經營利潤來
    major businesses. By operating profit, the animal nutrition segment       看，動物營養已成為我們最賺錢的業務分部
    has become one of the most profitable businesses that fuels our           之 一，並 成 為 我 們 高 速 增 長 的 引 擎，使 我 們
    high growth engine, transforming us into a formidable competitor in       在動物營養行業內成為令人敬畏的競爭對手。
    the animal nutrition industry. In contrast with our competitors, our      我們的產品系列在競爭對手中最為完整，包
    product portfolio is the most complete product line, which comprises      括 蘇 氨 酸、賴 氨 酸、色 氨 酸、纈 氨 酸 及 異 亮
    threonine, lysine, tryptophan, valine and isoleucine, thereby holding     氨酸，並佔據頭部位置。
    the leading position.

    From the perspective of our overall core competitiveness, our             從集團的整體核心競爭力來看，我們的優勢
    advantages depend on: 1) our leading bio-fermentation technology; 2)      在於：1）領先的生物發酵技術；2）整合資源
    our capability to integrate resources; and 3) our capability to control   的能力；及 3）生產規模及核心成本的控制能力。
    production scale and core costs.

    Focus and risk diversification represent our business philosophy,         我們的經營理念是專注與分散風險。我們專
    based on which, we focus on our core bio-fermentation technology          注於我們最核心的生物發酵技術併採取多元
    and adopted the strategy for diversity development to diversify risks.    化 的 發 展 策 略，從 而 分 散 風 險。我 們 有 多 元
    Due to our strategy to diversify the product portfolio, production        化 的 產 品 組 合、多 元 化 的 生 產 基 地、多 元 化
    base, and market and customer base, we have mitigated the risks           的市場及客戶群體。所以我們降低了單一產
    arising from the reliance on a single product, a single production        品、單 一 生 產 基 地、單 一 市 場 及 客 戶 群 體 所
    base, and a single market and customer base. This also explains           帶來的風險。這也是為甚麼我們加快全球資
    that we strive to become the world’s leading corn biochemical             源整合及國際化的計劃，致力成為全球領先

accelerating the establishment of a more complete international           際 化 生 產 及 銷 售 網 絡 ; 及 (2) 確 保 現 有 生 產 經

of the existing production and operation capacity to maintain and         市場的領導地位，同步推進「做大」和「做強」。
consolidate our leading position in the Chinese market so that we

                                                                             MANAGEMENT REVIEW
                                                                                    管理層回顧


Market Overview                                                           市場概覽
During the year, the market was still affected by the COVID pandemic.     年 內，市 場 仍 受 新 冠 疫 情 影 響。市 場 需 求 尚
Market demand has not fully recovered to the pre-pandemic level. In       未 完 全 恢 復 到 疫 情 前 的 水 平。此 外，市 場 受
addition, the market experienced the impacts from the unexpected          到突如其來的能耗碳排放雙控的影響，年內
dual controls over energy consumption and carbon emission, some           部分生產經營活動受到干擾。
production and operation activities were disrupted during the year.

The fierce competition in the industry in 2021 was evidenced by the       從行業競爭的角度來看，二零二一年行業競
high frequency of change and volatility of the prices of products. Such   爭非常激烈，從我們產品價格的漲跌頻率快
fierce competition was mainly due to the status of excess supply          及漲跌波幅大可以看出來。競爭激烈的主要
over demand in the market as a result of the significant increase in      原因是近年大型的玉米深加工企業在玉米資
production capacity, which was caused by the new facilities built by      源豐富的中國東北建設新設施，產能顯著擴
large corn processing enterprises in Northeast China, a region with       張，導致市場供大於求。
abundant corn resources, in recent years.

Driven by the high cost of raw materials, prices of key products, such    在原材料成本高企的推動下，味精、蘇氨酸、
as MSG, threonine, lysine, high-end amino acid and xanthan gum            賴 氨 酸、高 檔 氨 基 酸、黃 原 膠 等 主 要 產 品 價
products increased significantly during the year (especially in the       格在年內（尤其是第四季度）大幅上漲。
fourth quarter).

The corn price increased significantly and maintained at a high level,    玉米價格大幅上漲並維持在高位，主要由於
which was mainly due to the increased feed demand, increased corn         飼料需求增加、玉米生產成本上升（例如肥料）
production cost (such as fertiliser) and demand recovery from the         及玉米深加工的需求復甦。
corn processing sector.

In 2021, China had approximately 300 million tonnes of corn supply,       官方數據顯示，二零二一年中國玉米供應量
of which approximately 272 million tonnes were self-produced and          約為 300 百萬噸，其中自產約 272 百萬噸，進
approximately 28 million tonnes were imported, according to the           口約 28 百萬噸。
official data.

The coal price also increased significantly during the year, especially   煤炭價格在年內亦大幅上漲，尤其是在二零
after the supply shortage happened in September 2021, which               二一年九月發生供應短缺情況之後，此導致
resulted in a power shortage in China. However, some market players       中 國 電 力 短 缺。然 而，部 分 玉 米 深 加 工 市 場
in corn processing have their own power plants in their production        參與者在其生產基地設有自身的發電廠，盡
bases and can access to low-cost local coal resources. So, the            享 當 地 低 成 本 煤 炭 資 源 之 利。因 此，影 響 降
impact was minimised.                                                     至最低。

For the year ended 31 December 2021, the Group’s revenue                    截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止年度，本
increased by 29.1% to approximately RMB21,539.8 million as                  集團的收入較二零二零年增加 29.1% 至約人
compared to 2020, primarily due to increased revenue contribution           民 幣 21,539,800,000 元，主 要 由 於 食 品 添 加
from food additives, animal nutrition and high-end amino acid.              劑、動物營養及高檔氨基酸產品的收入貢獻
                                                                            增加所致。

The Group’s overall gross profit increased by 38.7% to approximately        於二零二一年，本集團的整體毛利較二零二
RMB3,905.3 million in 2021 as compared to 2020, primarily due               零 年 增 加 38.7% 至 約 人 民 幣 3,905,300,000
to the increases in the gross profit of animal nutrition and high-end       元，主要由於動物營養及高檔氨基酸產品的
amino acid products.                                                        毛利提高。

Selling and marketing expenses increased by 1.2% to approximately           於二零二一年，銷售及市場推廣開支較二零
RMB1,401.2 million in 2021 as compared to 2020.                             二零年增加 1.2% 至約人民幣 1,401,200,000 元。

Administrative expenses increased by 16.2% to approximately                 於二零二一年，行政開支較二零二零年增加
RMB949.0 million in 2021 as compared to 2020.                               16.2% 至約人民幣 949,000,000 元。

            strategy (i.e. risk response) for each key risk;                         風險管理策略（即風險回應）；

            the risk response are operated effectively;                              應行之有效；

            and report to Risk Management Team.                                      險管理小組匯報。

risk management functions. No significant areas of concern that may       概無識別出任何可能影響本公司實現戰略目
affect the Company to achieve strategic goals have been identified.       標的重大關注範疇。

To the Shareholders of Fufeng Group Limited                                         致阜豐集團有限公司股東
(incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)                        （於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）


OPINION                                                                             意見
What we have audited                                                                我們已審計的內容
The consolidated financial statements of Fufeng Group Limited (the                  阜豐集團有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公司」）及其
“Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”), which are set out on                 附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）列載於第 68 至
pages 68 to 200, comprise:                                                          200 頁的合併財務報表，包括：


•     the consolidated balance sheet as at 31 December 2021;                        •    於二零二一年十二月三十一日的合併資
                                                                                         產負債表；
•     the consolidated income statement for the year then ended;                    •    截至該日止年度的合併利潤表；
•     the consolidated statement of comprehensive income for the                    •    截至該日止年度的合併綜合收益表；
      year then ended;
•     the consolidated statement of changes in equity for the year                  •    截至該日止年度的合併權益變動表；
      then ended;
•     the consolidated cash flow statement for the year then ended;                 •    截至該日止年度的合併現金流量表；及
      and
•     the notes to the consolidated financial statements, which include             •    合併財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策
      significant accounting policies and other explanatory information.                 及其他解釋說明。


Our opinion                                                                         我們的意見
In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and               我們認為，合併財務報表已根據香港會計師
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31              公會（
                                                                                      「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香港財務報
December 2021, and of its consolidated financial performance and                    告準則》（「香港財務報告準則」）真實而中肯地
its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance                   反映了 貴集團於二零二一年十二月三十一
with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by                   日的合併財務狀況及其截至該日止年度的合

statements.                                                               註 5。

Revenue from sales of goods amounted to RMB21,540 million for the         截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止年度，商
year ended 31 December 2021. Revenue is recognised when control           品銷售收入為人民幣 215.4 億元。相關商品控
of the underlying products has been transferred to the customers.         制權轉移至客戶時，確認相關收入。

We focused on this area due to the huge volume of revenue                 我們考慮到於眾多不同地點與眾多客戶進行
transactions generated from sales of numerous kinds of products           交 易，銷 售 多 種 商 品 且 交 易 量 巨 大，我 們 對
to a large number of customers that occurred in many different            該 領 域 重 點 關 注。對 於 出 口 銷 售，商 品 控 制
locations. In relation to export sales, it usually takes more time for    權轉移給購貨方通常比國內銷售耗時更久，
control of products to pass over to customers than domestic sales.        故收入是否已確認在正確報告期間存在潛在
There is potential risk of misstatement in relation to whether revenue    錯報風險。
is recognised in the correct reporting periods.

How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter                              我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項
We evaluated and validated management’s key controls that                 我們對 貴集團銷售流程中管理層現有的關
are present in the Group’s sales process from end-to-end, from            鍵控制進行評估及驗證，包括從客戶訂單批
customer order’s approval, sales recording, all the way through to        准、記錄銷售一直到現金收據及客戶記錄對
reconciliations with cash receipts and customers’ records.                賬整個過程。

We conducted testing of sales revenue recorded covering different         我們對不同產品、地點及客戶的銷售收入執
products, locations and customers, by examining the relevant              行 了 測 試，檢 查 銷 售 相 關 支 持 性 文 件，包 括
supporting documents including customer orders, goods delivery            客 戶 訂 單、發 貨 單 及 報 關 單。此 外，我 們 對
notes and customs declaration notices. In addition, we confirmed          資產負債表日的特定客戶應收賬款餘額及全
certain customers’ receivable balances at the balance sheet date          年交易額進行了發函確認，如果回函金額同
and their transaction amounts during the year, and tested the             賬面金額存在差異，則對差異調節表進行測
reconciliations between the book amounts and confirmed amounts            試。以上抽樣測試項目系通過考慮交易金額、
if these were different. The items tested were selected on a sample       交易性質及客戶特徵等基礎上挑選的。
basis by considering the amount, nature and characteristics of those
customers.

Our work also included testing of a sample of manual revenue-             我們的工作還包括通過詢問管理層該些分錄
related journal entries by inquiring management of the nature of these    的性質以及檢查其支持性文件來測試被抽樣
journals and inspection of the supporting documents.                      的與收入相關的手工分錄。

Furthermore, one of our focused audit efforts was testing export          並且，我們的審計重點之一是對資產負債表
sales transactions that took place shortly before and after the balance   日前後不久發生的出口銷售交易進行截止性
sheet date, by reconciling recognised revenue with the goods delivery     測 試，通 過 檢 查 發 貨 單 和 報 關 單，與 已 確 認
notes and customs declaration notices to assess whether revenue           收入進行對比，評估收入是否確認在正確報
was recognised in the correct reporting periods.                          告期間。

We found the Group’s sales transactions being tested were                 我 們 認 為，已 測 試 的 貴 集 團 銷 售 交 易 的 確
recognised in a manner consistent with the Group’s revenue                認同 貴集團收入確認的會計政策保持一致。
recognition accounting policy.

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                    1. 一般資料
  Fufeng Group Limited and its subsidiaries manufacture and sell           阜豐集團有限公司及其附屬公司從事製
  fermentation-based food additives, biochemical products and              造及銷售發酵食品添加劑、生化產品及
  starch-based products. The Group has manufacturing plants                澱粉產品。本集團於中國山東省、陝西省、
  in Shandong Province, Shaanxi Province, Jiangsu Province,                江 蘇 省、黑 龍 江 省、內 蒙 古 自 治 區 及 新
  Heilongjiang Province, Inner Mongolia Autonomous Region and              疆維吾爾自治區設有生產廠房，主要向
  Xinjiang Uygur Autonomous Region of the PRC and sells mainly             中國客戶進行銷售。
  to customers located in the PRC.

  The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in the           本公司為於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公
  Cayman Islands. The address of its registered office is Cricket          司，其註冊辦事處地址為 Cricket Square,
  Square, Hutchins Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman KY1-                 Hutchins Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand
  1111, Cayman Islands.                                                    Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman Islands。

  The Company has its shares listed on The Stock Exchange of               本公司股份在香港聯合交易所有限公司
  Hong Kong Limited.                                                       上市。

  These consolidated financial statements are presented in RMB,            除另有註明外，此等合併財務報表以人

            expenditure, the latter being allocated on the basis of              者乃根據正常運營能力分配。存貨
            normal operating capacity. It excludes borrowing costs.              不包括借貸成本。成本以加權平均
            Cost is determined using the weighted average method.                法釐定。可變現淨值指於日常業務
            Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the           過程的估計售價減去銷售所需的估
            ordinary course of business, less the estimated costs                計成本。
            necessary to make the sale.

      or have been shipped on board for overseas sales, the                已根據銷售合約接納產品，接納條
      risks of obsolescence and loss have been transferred                 文已失效，或本集團有客觀證據證
      to the customer, and either the customer has accepted                明已達成接納之所有條件時即交付。
      the products in accordance with the sales contract, the
      acceptance provisions have lapsed, or the Group has
      objective evidence that all criteria for acceptance have been
      satisfied.

           The lease payments are discounted using the interest                 租賃付款使用租賃中內含的利率進
           rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily           行貼現。倘無法輕易確定該利率（為
           determined, which is generally the case for leases in the            本 集 團 租 賃 的 一 般 情 況），則 使 用
           group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,              承租人的增量借款利率，即個別承
           being the rate that the lessee would have to pay to borrow           租人在類似經濟環境中按類似條款
           the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in           及條件借入獲得與使用權資產具有
           a similar economic environment with similar terms and                類似價值的資產所需資金而必須支
           conditions.                                                          付的利率。

           To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:              為釐定增量借款利率，本集團應：

           •    where possible, uses recent third-party financing               •   在可能情況下，以承租人最近
                received by the individual lessee as a starting point,              收到的第三方融資為起點，並
                adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                 進行調整以反映融資條件自收
                since third party financing was received,                           到第三方融資後的變化，

           •    uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free           •   對於近期未獲得第三方融資的
                interest rate adjusted for credit risk for leases held by           本集團持有的租賃，採用無風
                the Group, which does not have recent third party                   險利率為起點的累加法，並按
                financing, and                                                      照租賃的信用風險進行調整，
                                                                                    及

                country, currency and security.                                     租賃期、國家、貨幣及抵押。

           Lease payment is allocated between the principal and                 租賃付款額於本金及融資費用之間

           over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic           入損益，以按照固定的周期性利率
           rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability for       對各期間負債餘額計算利息。
           each period.

       3.2 Capital management                                                  3.2 資本管理
           (a)   Risk management                                                 (a)   風險管理
                 The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                   本集團的資本管理目標為

                 •      safeguard their ability to continue as a going                 •   保障其按持續經營基準繼
                        concern, so that they can continue to provide                      續營運的能力，因此彼等
                        returns for shareholders and benefits for other                    可繼續為股東帶來回報，
                        stakeholders, and                                                  同時兼顧其他權益持有人
                                                                                           的利益，及

                 •      maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce                •   維持最佳資本結構以減低
                        the cost of capital.                                               資金成本。



                 In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure,                 為維持或調整資本架構，本集
                 the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid                     團或會調整支付予股東的股息
                 to shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue                金 額、向 股 東 發 還 資 本、發 行
                 new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                             新股或出售資產以減低債務。

                 The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing                本集團根據資產負債比率監察
                 ratio. This ratio is equal to total borrowings divided by             其資本。該比率相等於借貸總
                 total assets at the end of corresponding year.                        額除以上各對應年度年結日資
                                                                                       產總值。

                 The Group’s strategy is to maintain the gearing ratio                 本集團的策略為將資產負債比
                 below 40% (2020: 40%).The gearing ratios at 31                        率 維 持 於 40% 以 下（二 零 二 零
                 December 2021 and 2020 were as follows:                               年：40% ）。於 二 零 二 一 年 及

                                                                                            出。

                       Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments                      第一級：在活躍市場上買
                       traded in active markets (such as publicly traded                     賣 的 金 融 工 具（如 公 開 買

                       bid price. These instruments are included in level                    場報價為當時買盤價。該
                       1.                                                                    等工具計入第一級。

                       that are not traded in an active market (for                          買 賣 的 金 融 工 具（例 如 場
                       example, over-the-counter derivatives) is                             外 衍 生 工 具）的 公 平 值 採
                       determined using valuation techniques which                           用估值技術釐定。該等估
                       maximise the use of observable market data                            值技術盡量採用可觀察市
                       and rely as little as possible on entity-specific                     場數據，並盡可能不倚賴
                       estimates. If all significant inputs required to                      實體特定估計。倘計算工
                       fair value an instrument are observable, the                          具公平值所需的重大輸入

                                                                                             計入第二級。

       based on historical experience and other factors, including         經驗及其他因素作基礎，包括在目前情
       expectations of future events that are believed to be reasonable    況下對未來事件的合理預測。
       under the circumstances.

25. OTHER RESERVES (Continued)                                         25. 其他儲備（續）
   (a) Capital reserve                                                   (a) 資本儲備
       It mainly represents reserve arising from the Group’s                資本儲備主要指本集團於二零零六
       reorganisation completed in July 2006.                               年七月完成重組後產生的儲備。

   (b) Statutory reserve                                                 (b) 法定儲備
       In accordance with the PRC regulations and the articles              根據中國規例及組成本集團的旗下
       of the association of the PRC companies comprising the               各中國公司的組織章程細則規定，
       Group, before distributing the net profit of each year, each         在分派各年純利前，每間在中國註
       of the companies registered in the PRC is required to                冊的公司均須按中國會計準則規定，
       set aside 10% of its statutory net profit for the year after         於對銷任何上一年度虧損後，將該
       offsetting any prior year’s losses as determined under the           年度的法定純利 10% 撥入法定盈餘
       PRC accounting regulations to the statutory surplus reserve          儲備基金。倘該儲備的結餘達到各
       fund. When the balance of such reserve reaches 50% of                公司股本的 50%，其後可自行決定
       each company’s share capital, any further appropriation is           任何撥調。法定盈餘儲備基金可用
       optional. The statutory surplus reserve fund can be utilised         以對銷過往年度虧損或發行紅股，
       to offset prior years’ losses or to issue bonus shares,              惟有關儲備結餘不少於發行紅股後
       provided that the balance of such reserve is not less than           實體註冊資本的 25%。
       25% of the entity’s registered capital after the bonus issue.
